# Claim ledger: limits-thm level 4, concept audit round 2 (blind)

Scope: l14 to l19, review-4, homework-4, problems-4 at commit f65b7657. One row per string that states or uses a claim: prose, questions, options and their reasons, hints, feedback lines, explanations, accept lines, summary lines, derive lines, graded keys with their wrong options, code tests, model answers and rubric lines. Every row was read and judged by hand. Rows of one screen share the basis written for that screen; a row with its own note carries it. Numbers, slips and simulations are in `docs/reports/limits-thm-L4-concepts-r2.py` (710 checks, exit 0). CB is Casella and Berger, *Statistical Inference*, 2nd edition.

Rows: 1585. Verdicts: correct 1568, wrong 0, imprecise 17, misleading 0, unverified 0.

| problems-4.json | items[3].parts[1].feedback.correct | $M''(s) = 24(1 - 2s)^{-4}$, so $E[D^2] = 24$ and $\operatorname{Var}(D) = 24 - 4^2 = 8$. | correct | Chi-square(4) = Gamma(2, rate 1/2), mgf (1-2s)^-2, tail e^{-d/2}(1+d/2) (scipy). MC. |
| problems-4.json | items[3].parts[1].feedback.generic | Not quite. Find $M''(0) = E[D^2]$, then take away $M'(0)^2$. | correct | Chi-square(4) = Gamma(2, rate 1/2), mgf (1-2s)^-2, tail e^{-d/2}(1+d/2) (scipy). MC. |
| problems-4.json | items[3].parts[1].feedback.byKey.second-moment | That is $E[D^2] = M''(0)$. Take away $M'(0)^2 = 16$. | correct | Chi-square(4) = Gamma(2, rate 1/2), mgf (1-2s)^-2, tail e^{-d/2}(1+d/2) (scipy). MC. |
| problems-4.json | items[3].parts[1].feedback.byKey.one-term | That is $\operatorname{Var}(Z^2)$ for one term. Four independent terms give 8. | correct | Chi-square(4) = Gamma(2, rate 1/2), mgf (1-2s)^-2, tail e^{-d/2}(1+d/2) (scipy). MC. |
| problems-4.json | items[3].parts[1].feedback.byKey.sd | That is the sd, $\sqrt{8}$. The question asks for the variance. | correct | Chi-square(4) = Gamma(2, rate 1/2), mgf (1-2s)^-2, tail e^{-d/2}(1+d/2) (scipy). MC. |
| problems-4.json | items[3].parts[1].explain.text | $M'(s) = 4(1 - 2s)^{-3}$ and $M''(s) = 24(1 - 2s)^{-4}$. So $E[D] = 4$, $E[D^2] = 24$ and $\operatorname{Var}(D) = 8$, … | correct | Chi-square(4) = Gamma(2, rate 1/2), mgf (1-2s)^-2, tail e^{-d/2}(1+d/2) (scipy). MC. |
| problems-4.json | items[3].parts[2].question | Part 2 used $M_D(s) = (1 - 2s)^{-2}$. An MGF that is finite near 0 pins down its distribution. Which is $D$? | correct | Chi-square(4) = Gamma(2, rate 1/2), mgf (1-2s)^-2, tail e^{-d/2}(1+d/2) (scipy). MC. |

| problems-4.json | items[3].parts[3].question | Using part 3's answer, $D$ is the wait for the 2nd arrival at rate $\tfrac12$. What is $P(D > 8)$? Give your answer to … | correct | Chi-square(4) = Gamma(2, rate 1/2), mgf (1-2s)^-2, tail e^{-d/2}(1+d/2) (scipy). MC. |
| problems-4.json | items[3].parts[3].widget.answer | key 0.0916 tol {'abs': 0.0005}; wrong exponential=0.0183, three-terms=0.2381, rate-one=0.003 | correct | Chi-square(4) = Gamma(2, rate 1/2), mgf (1-2s)^-2, tail e^{-d/2}(1+d/2) (scipy). MC. |
| problems-4.json | items[3].parts[3].feedback.correct | $D > 8$ when fewer than 2 arrivals come by 8, a Poisson count of mean 4: $e^{-4}(1 + 4) \approx 0.0916$. | correct | Chi-square(4) = Gamma(2, rate 1/2), mgf (1-2s)^-2, tail e^{-d/2}(1+d/2) (scipy). MC. |
| problems-4.json | items[3].parts[3].feedback.generic | Not quite. Turn the wait into a count: how many arrivals at rate $\tfrac12$ come by time 8? | correct | Chi-square(4) = Gamma(2, rate 1/2), mgf (1-2s)^-2, tail e^{-d/2}(1+d/2) (scipy). MC. |
| problems-4.json | items[3].parts[3].feedback.byKey.exponential | That is $e^{-4}$, the chance of no arrival. The 2nd arrival is late if there are 0 or 1. | correct | Chi-square(4) = Gamma(2, rate 1/2), mgf (1-2s)^-2, tail e^{-d/2}(1+d/2) (scipy). MC. |
| problems-4.json | items[3].parts[3].feedback.byKey.three-terms | That is $P(N \le 2)$, for the 3rd arrival. $D$ is the 2nd: $P(N \le 1)$. | correct | Chi-square(4) = Gamma(2, rate 1/2), mgf (1-2s)^-2, tail e^{-d/2}(1+d/2) (scipy). MC. |
| problems-4.json | items[3].parts[3].feedback.byKey.rate-one | That uses rate 1. The rate is $\tfrac12$, so the count by 8 has mean 4. | correct | Chi-square(4) = Gamma(2, rate 1/2), mgf (1-2s)^-2, tail e^{-d/2}(1+d/2) (scipy). MC. |
| problems-4.json | items[3].parts[3].explain.text | The count of arrivals by 8 is Poisson with mean $8 \times \tfrac12 = 4$. The 2nd arrival is after 8 when that count is … | correct | Chi-square(4) = Gamma(2, rate 1/2), mgf (1-2s)^-2, tail e^{-d/2}(1+d/2) (scipy). MC. |

| problems-4.json | items[3].parts[4].feedback.byKey.normal | That is $4 + 1.645\sqrt{8} \approx 8.65$, rounded up: a normal approximation. At 9 the true tail is 0.0611. | correct | Chi-square(4) = Gamma(2, rate 1/2), mgf (1-2s)^-2, tail e^{-d/2}(1+d/2) (scipy). MC. |
| problems-4.json | items[3].parts[4].feedback.byKey.ten-per-cent | At 8 the tail is 0.0916, from part 4. That flags over 9% of good parts. | correct | Chi-square(4) = Gamma(2, rate 1/2), mgf (1-2s)^-2, tail e^{-d/2}(1+d/2) (scipy). MC. |
